/**
 * Parses an identifier expression
 *
 * @param parser  Parser object
 * @param context Context masks
 */
function parseIdentifier(parser: Parser, context: Context): ESTree.Identifier {
  const { tokenValue, tokenStart } = parser;

  const allowRegex = tokenValue === 'await' && (parser.getToken() & Token.IsEscaped) === 0;
  nextToken(parser, context | (allowRegex ? Context.AllowRegExp : 0));

  return parser.finishNode<ESTree.Identifier>(
    {
      type: 'Identifier',
      name: tokenValue,
    },
    tokenStart,
  );
}

type StringOrNumberLiteral = ESTree.StringLiteral | ESTree.BigIntLiteral | ESTree.NumericLiteral;

/**
 * Parses an literal expression such as string literal
 *
 * @param parser  Parser object
 * @param context Context masks
 */
function parseLiteral<T extends StringOrNumberLiteral = StringOrNumberLiteral>(parser: Parser, context: Context): T {
  const { tokenValue, tokenRaw, tokenStart } = parser;
  if (parser.getToken() === Token.BigIntLiteral) {
    return parseBigIntLiteral(parser, context) as T;
  }

  const node: ESTree.StringLiteral | ESTree.NumericLiteral = {
    type: 'Literal',
    value: tokenValue,
  };
  if (parser.options.raw) {
    node.raw = tokenRaw;
  }

  nextToken(parser, context);
  parser.assignable = AssignmentKind.CannotAssign;

  return parser.finishNode(node, tokenStart) as T;
}

/**
 * Parses either expression or labeled statement
 *
 * @param parser  Parser object
 * @param context Context masks
 * @param allowFuncDecl Allow / disallow func statement
 */

function parseExpressionOrLabelledStatement(
  parser: Parser,
  context: Context,
  scope: Scope | undefined,
  privateScope: PrivateScope | undefined,
  origin: Origin,
  labels: ESTree.Labels,
  allowFuncDecl: 0 | 1,
): ESTree.ExpressionStatement | ESTree.LabeledStatement {
  // ExpressionStatement | LabelledStatement ::
  //   Expression ';'
  //   Identifier ':' Statement
  //
  // ExpressionStatement[Yield] :
  //   [lookahead not in {{, function, class, let [}] Expression[In, ?Yield] ;

  const { tokenValue, tokenStart } = parser;
  const token = parser.getToken();

  let expr: ESTree.Expression;

  switch (token) {
    case Token.LetKeyword:
      expr = parseIdentifier(parser, context);
      if (context & Context.Strict) parser.report(Errors.UnexpectedLetStrictReserved);

      // "let" followed by either "[", "{" or an identifier means a lexical
      // declaration, which should not appear here.
      // However, ASI may insert a line break before an identifier or a brace.
      if (parser.getToken() === Token.LeftBracket) parser.report(Errors.RestrictedLetProduction);

      break;

    default:
      expr = parsePrimaryExpression(parser, context, privateScope, BindingKind.Empty, 0, 1, 0, 1, parser.tokenStart);
  }

  /** LabelledStatement[Yield, Await, Return]:
   *
   * ExpressionStatement | LabelledStatement ::
   * Expression ';'
   *   Identifier ':' Statement
   *
   * ExpressionStatement[Yield] :
   *   [lookahead not in {{, function, class, let [}] Expression[In, ?Yield] ;
   */
  if (token & Token.IsIdentifier && parser.getToken() === Token.Colon) {
    return parseLabelledStatement(
      parser,
      context,
      scope,
      privateScope,
      origin,
      labels,
      tokenValue,
      expr,
      token,
      allowFuncDecl,
      tokenStart,
    );
  }
  /** MemberExpression :
   *   1. PrimaryExpression
   *   2. MemberExpression [ AssignmentExpression ]
   *   3. MemberExpression . IdentifierName
   *   4. MemberExpression TemplateLiteral
   *
   * CallExpression :
   *   1. MemberExpression Arguments
   *   2. CallExpression ImportCall
   *   3. CallExpression Arguments
   *   4. CallExpression [ AssignmentExpression ]
   *   5. CallExpression . IdentifierName
   *   6. CallExpression TemplateLiteral
   *
   *  UpdateExpression ::
   *   ('++' | '--')? LeftHandSideExpression
   *
   */

  expr = parseMemberOrUpdateExpression(parser, context, privateScope, expr, 0, 0, tokenStart);

  /** AssignmentExpression :
   *   1. ConditionalExpression
   *   2. LeftHandSideExpression = AssignmentExpression
   *
   */

  expr = parseAssignmentExpression(parser, context, privateScope, 0, 0, tokenStart, expr as ESTree.ArgumentExpression);

  /** Sequence expression
   *
   * Note: The comma operator leads to a sequence expression which is not equivalent
   * to the ES Expression, but it's part of the ESTree specs:
   *
   * https://github.com/estree/estree/blob/master/es5.md#sequenceexpression
   *
   */
  if (parser.getToken() === Token.Comma) {
    expr = parseSequenceExpression(parser, context, privateScope, 0, tokenStart, expr);
  }

  /**
   * ExpressionStatement[Yield, Await]:
   *  [lookahead ∉ { {, function, async [no LineTerminator here] function, class, let [ }]Expression[+In, ?Yield, ?Await]
   */

  return parseExpressionStatement(parser, context, expr, tokenStart);
}

 * @param context Context masks
 * @param scope  Scope object

function parseBlock<T extends ESTree.BlockStatement | ESTree.StaticBlock = ESTree.BlockStatement>(

  type: T['type'] = 'BlockStatement',
): T {
  // Block ::
  //   '{' StatementList '}'

  const body: ESTree.Statement[] = [];

  consume(parser, context | Context.AllowRegExp, Token.LeftBrace);
  while (parser.getToken() !== Token.RightBrace) {
    body.push(

 * @param context Context masks
 */
function parseReturnStatement(
  parser: Parser,
  context: Context,
  privateScope: PrivateScope | undefined,
): ESTree.ReturnStatement {
  // ReturnStatement ::
  //   'return' [no line terminator] Expression? ';'

  if ((context & Context.InReturnContext) === 0) parser.report(Errors.IllegalReturn);

  const start = parser.tokenStart;
  nextToken(parser, context | Context.AllowRegExp);

  const argument =
    parser.flags & Flags.NewLine || parser.getToken() & Token.IsAutoSemicolon
      ? null
      : parseExpressions(parser, context, privateScope, 0, 1, parser.tokenStart);

  matchOrInsertSemicolon(parser, context | Context.AllowRegExp);

  return parser.finishNode<ESTree.ReturnStatement>(
    {
      type: 'ReturnStatement',
      argument,
    },
    start,
  );
}

/**
 * Parses an expression statement
 *
 * @see [Link](https://tc39.github.io/ecma262/#prod-ExpressionStatement)
 *
 * @param parser  Parser object
 * @param context Context masks
 * @param expression AST node
 * @param start
 */
function parseExpressionStatement(
  parser: Parser,
  context: Context,
  expression: ESTree.Expression,
  start: Location,
): ESTree.ExpressionStatement {
  matchOrInsertSemicolon(parser, context | Context.AllowRegExp);
  return parser.finishNode<ESTree.ExpressionStatement>(
    {
      type: 'ExpressionStatement',
      expression,
    },
    start,
  );
}

    // Otherwise it's a parse error.

function parseFunctionBody(
  parser: Parser,
  context: Context,
  scope: Scope | undefined,
  privateScope: PrivateScope | undefined,
  origin: Origin,
  funcNameToken: Token | undefined,
  functionScope: Scope | undefined,
): ESTree.BlockStatement {
  const { tokenStart } = parser;

  consume(parser, context | Context.AllowRegExp, Token.LeftBrace);

  const body: ESTree.Statement[] = [];

  if (parser.getToken() !== Token.RightBrace) {
    while (parser.getToken() === Token.StringLiteral) {
      const { index, tokenStart, tokenIndex, tokenValue } = parser;
      const token = parser.getToken();
      const expr = parseLiteral<ESTree.StringLiteral>(parser, context);
      if (isValidStrictMode(parser, index, tokenIndex, tokenValue)) {
        context |= Context.Strict;
        // TC39 deemed "use strict" directives to be an error when occurring
        // in the body of a function with non-simple parameter list, on
        // 29/7/2015. https://goo.gl/ueA7Ln
        if (parser.flags & Flags.NonSimpleParameterList) {
          throw new ParseError(tokenStart, parser.currentLocation, Errors.IllegalUseStrict);
        }

        if (parser.flags & Flags.Octal) {
          throw new ParseError(tokenStart, parser.currentLocation, Errors.StrictOctalLiteral);
        }

        if (parser.flags & Flags.EightAndNine) {
          throw new ParseError(tokenStart, parser.currentLocation, Errors.StrictEightAndNine);
        }

        functionScope?.reportScopeError();
      }
      body.push(parseDirective(parser, context, expr, token, tokenStart));
    }
    if (context & Context.Strict) {
      if (funcNameToken) {
        if ((funcNameToken & Token.IsEvalOrArguments) === Token.IsEvalOrArguments) {
          parser.report(Errors.StrictEvalArguments);
        }
        if ((funcNameToken & Token.FutureReserved) === Token.FutureReserved) {
          parser.report(Errors.StrictFunctionName);
        }
      }
      if (parser.flags & Flags.StrictEvalArguments) parser.report(Errors.StrictEvalArguments);
      if (parser.flags & Flags.HasStrictReserved) parser.report(Errors.UnexpectedStrictReserved);
    }
  }

  parser.flags =
    (parser.flags | Flags.StrictEvalArguments | Flags.HasStrictReserved | Flags.Octal | Flags.EightAndNine) ^
    (Flags.StrictEvalArguments | Flags.HasStrictReserved | Flags.Octal | Flags.EightAndNine);

  parser.destructible = (parser.destructible | DestructuringKind.Yield) ^ DestructuringKind.Yield;

  while (parser.getToken() !== Token.RightBrace) {
    body.push(parseStatementListItem(parser, context, scope, privateScope, Origin.TopLevel, {}) as ESTree.Statement);
  }

  consume(
    parser,
    origin & (Origin.Arrow | Origin.Declaration) ? context | Context.AllowRegExp : context,
    Token.RightBrace,
  );

  parser.flags &= ~(Flags.NonSimpleParameterList | Flags.Octal | Flags.EightAndNine);

  if (parser.getToken() === Token.Assign) parser.report(Errors.CantAssignTo);

STEP 76 CALL 1 RETURN 0
COMMAND:
sed -n '2680,2780p' src/parser.ts
OUTPUT:
          ),
        );

        if (parser.getToken() !== Token.RightBrace) consume(parser, context, Token.Comma);
      }

      consume(parser, context, Token.RightBrace);

      if (consumeOpt(parser, context, Token.FromKeyword)) {
        //  The left hand side can't be a keyword where there is no
        // 'from' keyword since it references a local binding.
        if (parser.getToken() !== Token.StringLiteral) parser.report(Errors.InvalidExportImportSource, 'Export');

        source = parseLiteral<ESTree.StringLiteral>(parser, context);

        attributes = parseImportAttributes(parser, context);

        if (scope) {
          tmpExportedNames.forEach((n) => parser.declareUnboundVariable(n));
        }
      } else {
        if (hasLiteralLocal) {
          parser.report(Errors.InvalidExportReference);
        }

        if (scope) {
          tmpExportedNames.forEach((n) => parser.declareUnboundVariable(n));
          tmpExportedBindings.forEach((b) => parser.addBindingToExports(b));
        }
      }

      matchOrInsertSemicolon(parser, context | Context.AllowRegExp);

      break;
    }

    case Token.Decorator:
    case Token.ClassKeyword:
      declaration = parseClassDeclaration(parser, context, scope, undefined, HoistedClassFlags.Export);
      break;
    case Token.FunctionKeyword:
      declaration = parseFunctionDeclaration(
        parser,
        context,
        scope,
        undefined,
        Origin.TopLevel,
        1,
        HoistedFunctionFlags.Export,
        0,
        parser.tokenStart,
      );
      break;

    case Token.LetKeyword:
      declaration = parseLexicalDeclaration(parser, context, scope, undefined, BindingKind.Let, Origin.Export);
      break;
    case Token.ConstKeyword:
      declaration = parseLexicalDeclaration(parser, context, scope, undefined, BindingKind.Const, Origin.Export);
      break;
    case Token.VarKeyword:
      declaration = parseVariableStatement(parser, context, scope, undefined, Origin.Export);
      break;
    case Token.AsyncKeyword: {
      const { tokenStart } = parser;

      nextToken(parser, context);

      if ((parser.flags & Flags.NewLine) === 0 && parser.getToken() === Token.FunctionKeyword) {
        declaration = parseFunctionDeclaration(
          parser,
          context,
          scope,
          undefined,
          Origin.TopLevel,
          1,
          HoistedFunctionFlags.Export,
          1,
          tokenStart,
        );
        break;
      }
    }
    // falls through
    default:
      parser.report(Errors.UnexpectedToken, KeywordDescTable[parser.getToken() & Token.Type]);
  }

  const node: ESTree.ExportNamedDeclaration = {
    type: 'ExportNamedDeclaration',
    declaration,
    specifiers,
    source,
    attributes: attributes,
  };

  return parser.finishNode(node, start);
}

 * @param parser  Parser object
 * @param context Context masks
 */
export function scanIdentifier(parser: Parser, context: Context, isValidAsKeyword: 0 | 1): Token {
  while (isIdPart[advanceChar(parser)]);
  parser.tokenValue = parser.source.slice(parser.tokenIndex, parser.index);

  if (parser.currentChar !== Chars.Backslash && parser.currentChar <= 0x7e) {
    const token = getOwnProperty(descKeywordTable, parser.tokenValue);
    if (token === Token.UsingKeyword && !parser.options.next) return Token.Identifier;
    return token ?? Token.Identifier;
  }
  return scanIdentifierSlowCase(parser, context, 0, isValidAsKeyword);
}

/**
 * Scans unicode identifier
 * For identifier starts with unicode escape.
 *
 * @param parser  Parser object
 * @param context Context masks
  }

  if (parser.index <= parser.end) {
    parser.tokenValue += parser.source.slice(start, parser.index);
  }

  const { length } = parser.tokenValue;
  if (isValidAsKeyword && length >= 2 && length <= 11) {
    const token = getOwnProperty(descKeywordTable, parser.tokenValue);
    if (token === void 0) return Token.Identifier | (hasEscape ? Token.IsEscaped : 0);
    if (token === Token.UsingKeyword) {
      if (!parser.options.next) return Token.Identifier | (hasEscape ? Token.IsEscaped : 0);
      return hasEscape ? Token.AnyIdentifier | Token.IsEscaped : token;

/**
 * The core context, passed around everywhere as a simple immutable bit set
 */

  None = 0,
  Strict = 1 << 0,
  Module = 1 << 1, // Current code should be parsed as a module body
  InSwitch = 1 << 2,
  InGlobal = 1 << 3,
  InClass = 1 << 4,

  InAwaitContext = 1 << 11,
  InReturnContext = 1 << 12,
  InArgumentList = 1 << 13,
  InConstructor = 1 << 14,

  AllowNewTarget = 1 << 16,

}

/**
 * Masks to track the binding kind
 */
export const enum BindingKind {
  None = 0,
  ArgumentList = 1 << 0,
  Empty = 1 << 1,
  Variable = 1 << 2,
  Let = 1 << 3,
  Const = 1 << 4,
  Using = 1 << 5,
  AwaitUsing = 1 << 6,
  Class = 1 << 7,
  FunctionLexical = 1 << 8,
  FunctionStatement = 1 << 9,
  CatchPattern = 1 << 10,
  CatchIdentifier = 1 << 11,
  Async = 1 << 12,
  Generator = 1 << 12,
  AsyncFunctionLexical = Async | FunctionLexical,
  GeneratorFunctionLexical = Generator | FunctionLexical,
  AsyncGeneratorFunctionLexical = Async | Generator | FunctionLexical,
  CatchIdentifierOrPattern = CatchIdentifier | CatchPattern,
  LexicalOrFunction = Variable | FunctionLexical,
  LexicalBinding = Let | Const | Using | AwaitUsing | FunctionLexical | FunctionStatement | Class,
}

 * The masks to track where something begins. E.g. statements, declarations or arrows.

  // VariableDeclaration :
  //   BindingIdentifier Initializer opt
  //   BindingPattern Initializer
  //
  // VariableDeclarationNoIn :
  //   BindingIdentifier InitializerNoIn opt
  //   BindingPattern InitializerNoIn

  const { tokenStart } = parser;
  const token = parser.getToken();

  let init: ESTree.Expression | ESTree.BindingPattern | ESTree.Identifier | null = null;

  const isUsingKind = (kind & (BindingKind.Using | BindingKind.AwaitUsing)) !== 0;
  const id = parseBindingPattern(parser, context, scope, privateScope, kind, origin);

  if (isUsingKind && (token & Token.IsPatternStart) !== 0) {
    parser.report(Errors.UsingCannotHaveDestructuring);
  }

  if (parser.getToken() === Token.Assign) {
    nextToken(parser, context | Context.AllowRegExp);
    init = parseExpression(parser, context, privateScope, 1, 0, parser.tokenStart);
    if (origin & Origin.ForStatement || (token & Token.IsPatternStart) === 0) {
      // Lexical declarations in for-in / for-of loops can't be initialized

      if (isUsingKind && parser.getToken() === Token.InKeyword) {
        parser.report(Errors.UsingNotAllowedInForIn);
      }

      if (
        parser.getToken() === Token.OfKeyword ||
        (parser.getToken() === Token.InKeyword &&
          (token & Token.IsPatternStart || (kind & BindingKind.Variable) === 0 || context & Context.Strict))
      ) {
        throw new ParseError(
          tokenStart,
          parser.currentLocation,
          Errors.ForInOfLoopInitializer,
          parser.getToken() === Token.OfKeyword ? 'of' : 'in',
        );
      }
    }
    // Normal const declarations, and const declarations in for(;;) heads, must be initialized.
  } else if (
    isUsingKind
      ? (parser.getToken() & Token.IsInOrOf) !== Token.IsInOrOf
      : (kind & BindingKind.Const || (token & Token.IsPatternStart) > 0) &&
        (parser.getToken() & Token.IsInOrOf) !== Token.IsInOrOf
  ) {
    parser.report(
      isUsingKind
        ? Errors.UsingMissingInitializer
        : Errors.DeclarationMissingInitializer,
      isUsingKind
        ? kind & BindingKind.AwaitUsing
          ? 'await using'
          : 'using'
        : kind & BindingKind.Const
          ? 'const'
          : 'destructuring',
    );
  }

  return parser.finishNode<ESTree.VariableDeclarator>(
    {
      type: 'VariableDeclarator',
      id,
      init,
    },
    tokenStart,
  );
}

  newLine: boolean;
  hasSpaceBefore: boolean;

/**
 * Scans a number of tokens ahead without consuming them.
 */
function peekTokens(parser: Parser, context: Context, count: number): PeekedToken[] {
  const saved = {
    index: parser.index,
    line: parser.line,
    column: parser.column,
    tokenIndex: parser.tokenIndex,
    tokenColumn: parser.tokenColumn,
    tokenLine: parser.tokenLine,
    currentChar: parser.currentChar,
    tokenValue: parser.tokenValue,
    tokenRaw: parser.tokenRaw,
    tokenRegExp: parser.tokenRegExp,
  };

  const result: PeekedToken[] = [];
  let previousEnd = parser.index;

  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const startLine = parser.line;
    const token = scanSingleToken(parser, context, LexerState.None);
    result.push({
      token,
      newLine: parser.line > startLine,
      hasSpaceBefore: parser.tokenIndex > previousEnd,
    });
    previousEnd = parser.index;
  }

  parser.index = saved.index;
  parser.line = saved.line;
  parser.column = saved.column;
  parser.tokenIndex = saved.tokenIndex;
  parser.tokenColumn = saved.tokenColumn;
  parser.tokenLine = saved.tokenLine;
  parser.currentChar = saved.currentChar;
  parser.tokenValue = saved.tokenValue;
  parser.tokenRaw = saved.tokenRaw;
  parser.tokenRegExp = saved.tokenRegExp;

  return result;
}

/**
 * Checks whether the token after `using` starts a `using` declaration.
 */
function isUsingBindingToken(
  token: PeekedToken | undefined,
  next: PeekedToken | undefined,
  isFor: boolean,
  isAwait: boolean,
): boolean {
  if (!token || token.newLine) return false;

  if ((token.token & Token.IsIdentifier) === Token.IsIdentifier) {
    // In a for-of/for-await-of head, `for (using of ...)` is interpreted as an
    // expression headed by the identifier `using` unless the `of` is clearly a
    // binding identifier (followed by something other than another loop keyword).
    if (isFor && !isAwait && token.token === Token.OfKeyword) {
      if (next && (next.token === Token.OfKeyword || next.token === Token.InKeyword)) {
        return false;
      }
    }
    return true;
  }

  // `using [x] = null` and `using {x} = null` (with a space) are treated as
  // declarations, and then rejected because destructuring is not allowed.
  if ((token.token === Token.LeftBracket || token.token === Token.LeftBrace) && token.hasSpaceBefore) {
    return true;
  }

  return false;
}

/**
 * Detects `using` / `await using` declarations without consuming tokens.
 */
function isUsingDeclarationStart(parser: Parser, context: Context, isAwait: boolean, isFor: boolean): boolean {
  if (!parser.options.next) return false;

  if (isAwait) {
    if (parser.getToken() !== Token.AwaitKeyword) return false;
    const tokens = peekTokens(parser, context, 3);
    if (tokens.length < 2) return false;
    if (tokens[0].token !== Token.UsingKeyword || tokens[0].newLine) return false;
    return isUsingBindingToken(tokens[1], tokens[2], isFor, true);
  }

  if (parser.getToken() !== Token.UsingKeyword) return false;
  const tokens = peekTokens(parser, context, 2);
  return isUsingBindingToken(tokens[0], tokens[1], isFor, false);
}

/**
 * Parses a `using` or `await using` declaration.
 */
function parseUsingDeclaration(
  parser: Parser,
  context: Context,
  scope: Scope | undefined,
  privateScope: PrivateScope | undefined,
  kind: BindingKind,
  start: Location,
  isFor: boolean,
): ESTree.VariableDeclaration {
  if (kind & BindingKind.AwaitUsing) {
    nextToken(parser, context);
    consume(parser, context, Token.UsingKeyword);
  } else {
    nextToken(parser, context);
  }

  const declarations = parseVariableDeclarationList(
    parser,
    isFor ? context | Context.DisallowIn : context,
    scope,
    privateScope,
    kind,
    isFor ? Origin.ForStatement : Origin.None,
  );

  if (!isFor) matchOrInsertSemicolon(parser, context | Context.AllowRegExp);

  return parser.finishNode<ESTree.VariableDeclaration>(
    {
      type: \'VariableDeclaration\',
      kind: kind & BindingKind.AwaitUsing ? \'await using\' : \'using\',
      declarations,
    },
    start,
  );
}

/**
 * Parses a `using` / `await using` statement, or falls back to normal
 * statement parsing when `using`/`await` is used as an identifier.
 */
function parseUsingOrAwaitUsingStatement(
  parser: Parser,
  context: Context,
  scope: Scope | undefined,
  privateScope: PrivateScope | undefined,
  origin: Origin,
  labels: ESTree.Labels,
  isAwait: 0 | 1,
): ESTree.Statement {
  if (!isUsingDeclarationStart(parser, context, isAwait === 1, false)) {
    return parseStatement(parser, context, scope, privateScope, origin, labels, 1);
  }

  const start = parser.tokenStart;
  const kind = isAwait ? BindingKind.AwaitUsing : BindingKind.Using;

  if (isAwait && !((context & Context.InAwaitContext) !== 0 || (context & Context.Module && context & Context.InGlobal))) {
    parser.report(Errors.AwaitUsingOutsideAsync);
  }

  if ((context & Context.InGlobal) !== 0 && (context & Context.Module) === 0 && (origin & Origin.TopLevel) !== 0) {
    parser.report(Errors.UsingNotAllowedInGlobal);
  }

  if (context & Context.InSwitchCase) parser.report(Errors.UsingNotAllowedInSwitch);

  return parseUsingDeclaration(parser, context, scope, privateScope, kind, start, false);
}

/**
 * Scans a number of tokens ahead without consuming them.
 */
function peekTokens(parser: Parser, context: Context, count: number): PeekedToken[] {
  const saved = {
    index: parser.index,
    line: parser.line,
    column: parser.column,
    tokenIndex: parser.tokenIndex,
    tokenColumn: parser.tokenColumn,
    tokenLine: parser.tokenLine,
    currentChar: parser.currentChar,
    tokenValue: parser.tokenValue,
    tokenRaw: parser.tokenRaw,
    tokenRegExp: parser.tokenRegExp,
  };

  const result: PeekedToken[] = [];
  let previousEnd = parser.index;

  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const startLine = parser.line;
    const token = scanSingleToken(parser, context, LexerState.None);
    result.push({
      token,
      newLine: parser.line > startLine,

  }

  parser.tokenIndex = saved.tokenIndex;
  parser.tokenColumn = saved.tokenColumn;
  parser.tokenLine = saved.tokenLine;
  parser.currentChar = saved.currentChar;
  parser.tokenValue = saved.tokenValue;
  parser.tokenRaw = saved.tokenRaw;
  parser.tokenRegExp = saved.tokenRegExp;

}

function isUsingBindingToken(
  token: PeekedToken | undefined,
  next: PeekedToken | undefined,
  isFor: boolean,
  isAwait: boolean,

function isUsingDeclarationStart(parser: Parser, context: Context, isAwait: boolean, isFor: boolean): boolean {

 *
 * @param parser  Parser object
 * @param context Context masks
 * @param type Binding kind
 * @param origin Binding origin
 */
function parseVariableDeclarationList(
  parser: Parser,
  context: Context,
  scope: Scope | undefined,
  privateScope: PrivateScope | undefined,
  kind: BindingKind,
  origin: Origin,
): ESTree.VariableDeclarator[] {
  let bindingCount = 1;
  const list: ESTree.VariableDeclarator[] = [
    parseVariableDeclaration(parser, context, scope, privateScope, kind, origin),
  ];
  while (consumeOpt(parser, context, Token.Comma)) {
    bindingCount++;
    list.push(parseVariableDeclaration(parser, context, scope, privateScope, kind, origin));
  }

  if (bindingCount > 1 && origin & Origin.ForStatement && parser.getToken() & Token.IsInOrOf) {
    parser.report(Errors.ForInOfLoopMultiBindings, KeywordDescTable[parser.getToken() & Token.Type]);
  }
  return list;
}

/**
 * Parses variable declaration
 *
 * @see [Link](https://tc39.github.io/ecma262/#prod-VariableDeclaration)
 *
 * @param parser  Parser object
 * @param context Context masks
 */
function parseVariableDeclaration(
  parser: Parser,
  context: Context,
  scope: Scope | undefined,
  privateScope: PrivateScope | undefined,
  kind: BindingKind,
  origin: Origin,
): ESTree.VariableDeclarator {
  // VariableDeclaration :
  //   BindingIdentifier Initializer opt
  //   BindingPattern Initializer
  //
  // VariableDeclarationNoIn :
  //   BindingIdentifier InitializerNoIn opt
  //   BindingPattern InitializerNoIn

  const { tokenStart } = parser;
  const token = parser.getToken();

  let init: ESTree.Expression | ESTree.BindingPattern | ESTree.Identifier | null = null;

  const isUsingKind = (kind & (BindingKind.Using | BindingKind.AwaitUsing)) !== 0;
  const id = parseBindingPattern(parser, context, scope, privateScope, kind, origin);

  if (isUsingKind && (token & Token.IsPatternStart) !== 0) {
    parser.report(Errors.UsingCannotHaveDestructuring);
  }

  if (parser.getToken() === Token.Assign) {
    nextToken(parser, context | Context.AllowRegExp);
    init = parseExpression(parser, context, privateScope, 1, 0, parser.tokenStart);
    if (origin & Origin.ForStatement || (token & Token.IsPatternStart) === 0) {
      // Lexical declarations in for-in / for-of loops can't be initialized

      if (isUsingKind && parser.getToken() === Token.InKeyword) {
        parser.report(Errors.UsingNotAllowedInForIn);
      }

      if (
        parser.getToken() === Token.OfKeyword ||
        (parser.getToken() === Token.InKeyword &&
          (token & Token.IsPatternStart || (kind & BindingKind.Variable) === 0 || context & Context.Strict))
      ) {
        throw new ParseError(
          tokenStart,
          parser.currentLocation,
          Errors.ForInOfLoopInitializer,
          parser.getToken() === Token.OfKeyword ? 'of' : 'in',
        );
      }
    }
    // Normal const declarations, and const declarations in for(;;) heads, must be initialized.
  } else if (
    isUsingKind
      ? (parser.getToken() & Token.IsInOrOf) !== Token.IsInOrOf
      : (kind & BindingKind.Const || (token & Token.IsPatternStart) > 0) &&
        (parser.getToken() & Token.IsInOrOf) !== Token.IsInOrOf
  ) {
    parser.report(


STEP 94 CALL 1 RETURN 0
COMMAND:
cd /app && grep -n "function parseForStatement" src/parser.ts && sed -n '2010,2115p' src/parser.ts
OUTPUT:
2049:function parseForStatement(
    isUsingKind
      ? (parser.getToken() & Token.IsInOrOf) !== Token.IsInOrOf
      : (kind & BindingKind.Const || (token & Token.IsPatternStart) > 0) &&
        (parser.getToken() & Token.IsInOrOf) !== Token.IsInOrOf
  ) {
    parser.report(
      isUsingKind
        ? Errors.UsingMissingInitializer
        : Errors.DeclarationMissingInitializer,
      isUsingKind
        ? kind & BindingKind.AwaitUsing
          ? 'await using'
          : 'using'
        : kind & BindingKind.Const
          ? 'const'
          : 'destructuring',
    );
  }

  return parser.finishNode<ESTree.VariableDeclarator>(
    {
      type: 'VariableDeclarator',
      id,
      init,
    },
    tokenStart,
  );
}

/**
 * Parses either For, ForIn or ForOf statement
 *
 * @see [Link](https://tc39.github.io/ecma262/#sec-for-statement)
 * @see [Link](https://tc39.github.io/ecma262/#sec-for-in-and-for-of-statements)
 *
 * @param parser Parser object
 * @param context Context masks

 */
function parseForStatement(
  parser: Parser,
  context: Context,
  scope: Scope | undefined,
  privateScope: PrivateScope | undefined,
  labels: ESTree.Labels,
): ESTree.ForStatement | ESTree.ForInStatement | ESTree.ForOfStatement {
  const start = parser.tokenStart;

  nextToken(parser, context);

  const forAwait =
    ((context & Context.InAwaitContext) > 0 || ((context & Context.Module) > 0 && (context & Context.InGlobal) > 0)) &&
    consumeOpt(parser, context, Token.AwaitKeyword);

  consume(parser, context | Context.AllowRegExp, Token.LeftParen);

  scope = scope?.createChildScope(ScopeKind.ForStatement);

  let test: ESTree.Expression | null = null;
  let update: ESTree.Expression | null = null;
  let destructible: AssignmentKind | DestructuringKind = 0;
  let init = null;
  let isVarDecl =
    parser.getToken() === Token.VarKeyword ||
    parser.getToken() === Token.LetKeyword ||
    parser.getToken() === Token.ConstKeyword;
  let right;

  const { tokenStart } = parser;
  const token = parser.getToken();

  if (isVarDecl) {
    if (token === Token.LetKeyword) {
      init = parseIdentifier(parser, context);
      if (parser.getToken() & (Token.IsIdentifier | Token.IsPatternStart)) {
        if (parser.getToken() === Token.InKeyword) {
          if (context & Context.Strict) parser.report(Errors.DisallowedLetInStrict);
        } else {
          init = parser.finishNode<ESTree.VariableDeclaration>(
            {
              type: 'VariableDeclaration',
              kind: 'let',
              declarations: parseVariableDeclarationList(
                parser,
                context | Context.DisallowIn,
                scope,
                privateScope,
                BindingKind.Let,
                Origin.ForStatement,
              ),
            },
            tokenStart,
          );
        }

        parser.assignable = AssignmentKind.Assignable;
      } else if (context & Context.Strict) {
        parser.report(Errors.DisallowedLetInStrict);
      } else {
        isVarDecl = false;
        parser.assignable = AssignmentKind.Assignable;
        init = parseMemberOrUpdateExpression(parser, context, privateScope, init, 0, 0, tokenStart);

        // `for of` only allows LeftHandSideExpressions which do not start with `let`, and no other production matches
        if (parser.getToken() === Token.OfKeyword) parser.report(Errors.ForOfLet);
      }

/**
 * Parses a `using` / `await using` statement, or falls back to normal
 * statement parsing when `using`/`await` is used as an identifier.
 */
function parseUsingOrAwaitUsingStatement(
  parser: Parser,
  context: Context,
  scope: Scope | undefined,
  privateScope: PrivateScope | undefined,
  origin: Origin,
  labels: ESTree.Labels,
  isAwait: 0 | 1,
): ESTree.Statement {
  if (!isUsingDeclarationStart(parser, context, isAwait === 1, false)) {
    return parseStatement(parser, context, scope, privateScope, origin, labels, 1);
  }

  const start = parser.tokenStart;
  const kind = isAwait ? BindingKind.AwaitUsing : BindingKind.Using;

  if (isAwait && !((context & Context.InAwaitContext) !== 0 || (context & Context.Module && context & Context.InGlobal))) {
    parser.report(Errors.AwaitUsingOutsideAsync);
  }

  if ((context & Context.InGlobal) !== 0 && (context & Context.Module) === 0 && (origin & Origin.TopLevel) !== 0) {
    parser.report(Errors.UsingNotAllowedInGlobal);
  }

import { describe, it } from 'vitest';
import { parseSource } from '../src/parser';

function show(label: string, fn: () => unknown) {
  try {
    const ast = fn();
    console.log('OK', label, JSON.stringify(ast.body, null, 1)?.slice(0, 600));
  } catch (e: any) {
    console.log('ERR', label, e.message);
  }
}

describe('scratch using', () => {
  it('cases', () => {
    show('using in block', () => parseSource('{ using x = null; }', { next: true }));
    show('using script top', () => parseSource('using x = null;', { next: true }));
    show('using commonjs top', () => parseSource('using x = null;', { next: true, sourceType: 'commonjs' }));
    show('using module top', () => parseSource('using x = null;', { next: true, sourceType: 'module' }));
    show('await using script top', () => parseSource('await using x = null;', { next: true }));
    show('await using module top', () => parseSource('await using x = null;', { next: true, sourceType: 'module' }));
    show('await using async', () => parseSource('async function f(){ await using x = null; }', { next: true }));
    show('for-of using', () => parseSource('for (using x of []) {}', { next: true }));
    show('for-of await using async', () => parseSource('async function f(){ for (await using x of []) {} }', { next: true }));
    show('for await using module', () => parseSource('for await (using x of []) {}', { next: true, sourceType: 'module' }));
    show('for await await using module', () => parseSource('for await (await using x of []) {}', { next: true, sourceType: 'module' }));
    show('using of = regular for', () => parseSource('for (using of = null;;) break;', { next: true }));
    show('using of of for-of', () => parseSource('for (using of of []) {}', { next: true }));
    show('await using of of async', () => parseSource('async function f(){ for (await using of of []) {} }', { next: true }));
    show('missing init', () => parseSource('{ using x; }', { next: true }));
    show('destructure space', () => parseSource('{ using [a] = null; }', { next: true }));
    show('element access', () => parseSource('{ using[x] = null; }', { next: true }));
    show('using newline', () => parseSource('{ using\nlet = 1; }', { next: true }));
    show('for-in using', () => parseSource('for (using x in y) {}', { next: true }));
    show('using as expression module', () => parseSource('using = 1;', { next: true, sourceType: 'module' }));
    show('using[x] module', () => parseSource('using[x] = null;', { next: true, sourceType: 'module' }));
    show('switch case', () => parseSource('function f(){ switch(x){ case 1: using y = null; } }', { next: true }));
    show('switch block', () => parseSource('function f(){ switch(x){ case 1: { using y = null; } } }', { next: true }));

cd /app && cat > test/using.scratch2.test.ts <<'EOF'
import { describe, it } from 'vitest';
import { parseSource } from '../src/parser';

function show(label: string, fn: () => unknown) {
  try {
    const ast = fn();
    const b = ast.body as any[];
    console.log('OK', label, b.map((n: any) => n.type + (n.kind ? '(' + n.kind + ')' : '')).join(','));
  } catch (e: any) {
    console.log('ERR', label, e.message);
  }
}

describe('scratch2', () => {
  it('cases', () => {
    show('for (using of of [0,1,2])', () => parseSource('for (using of of [0, 1, 2]) {}', { next: true }));
    show('for (await using x of []) script', () => parseSource('for (await using x of []) {}', { next: true }));
    show('for await (using x of []) script', () => parseSource('for await (using x of []) {}', { next: true }));
    show('for await (using x of []) async', () => parseSource('async function f(){ for await (using x of []) {} }', { next: true }));
    show('await using module block', () => parseSource('{ await using x = null; }', { next: true, sourceType: 'module' }));
    show('await using non-async fn in module', () => parseSource('function f(){ await using x = null; }', { next: true, sourceType: 'module' }));
    show('using no next in block', () => parseSource('{ using x = null; }'));
    show('using no next assignment', () => parseSource('using = 1;'));
    show('await using no next async', () => parseSource('async function f(){ await using[x]; }'));
    show('await newline using module', () => parseSource('await\nusing x = null;', { next: true, sourceType: 'module' }));
    show('using newline module', () => parseSource('using\nx = null;', { next: true, sourceType: 'module' }));
    show('await using newline using async', () => parseSource('async function f(){ await using\nx = null; }', { next: true }));
    show('using escaped module', () => parseSource('\\u0075sing x = null;', { next: true, sourceType: 'module' }));
    show('export using', () => parseSource('export using x = null;', { next: true, sourceType: 'module' }));
    show('export await using', () => parseSource('export await using x = null;', { next: true, sourceType: 'module' }));
    show('multiple using', () => parseSource('{ using x = null, y = null; }', { next: true }));
    show('using missing second init', () => parseSource('{ using x = null, y; }', { next: true }));
    show('using in for-in with init', () => parseSource('for (using x = 1 in y) {}', { next: true }));
  });
});
EOF

import { describe, it } from 'vitest';
import { parseSource } from '../src/parser';

function show(label: string, fn: () => unknown) {
  try { fn(); console.log('OK', label); } catch (e: any) { console.log('ERR', label, e.message); }
}

describe('scratch3', () => {
  it('cases', () => {
    show('for (await using x in y) async', () => parseSource('async function f(){ for (await using x in y) {} }', { next: true }));
    show('await using missing init async', () => parseSource('async function f(){ await using x; }', { next: true }));
    show('await using destructure array async', () => parseSource('async function f(){ await using [a] = null; }', { next: true }));
    show('await using destructure object async', () => parseSource('async function f(){ await using {a} = null; }', { next: true }));
    show('using destructure object', () => parseSource('{ using {a} = null; }', { next: true }));
    show('using multi then pattern', () => parseSource('{ using x = null, [a] = null; }', { next: true }));
    show('await using element access async', () => parseSource('async function f(){ await using[x]; }', { next: true }));
    show('await using [x] space async', () => parseSource('async function f(){ await using [x] = null; }', { next: true }));
    show('await using no line terminator statement', () => parseSource('async function f(){ await using\nx = null; }', { next: true }));
    show('using label', () => parseSource('using: for(;;) break using;', { next: true }));
    show('using arrow', () => parseSource('var f = using => using;', { next: true }));
    show('using as for-in LHS', () => parseSource('var using; for (using in x) ;', { next: true }));
    show('using as for-of LHS', () => parseSource('var using; for (using of of [0,1,2]) ;', { next: true }));
    show('using in if body', () => parseSource('if (x) using y = null;', { next: true }));
    show('using in while body', () => parseSource('while (x) using y = null;', { next: true }));
    show('await using in if body async', () => parseSource('async function f(){ if (x) await using y = null; }', { next: true }));
  });
});
EOF

    // using declarations in blocks and function bodies
    { code: '{ using x = null; }', options: { next: true } },
    { code: 'function f() { using x = null; }', options: { next: true } },
    { code: '{ using x = null, y = null; }', options: { next: true } },
    // module top-level
    { code: 'using x = null;', options: { next: true, sourceType: 'module' } },
    { code: 'await using x = null;', options: { next: true, sourceType: 'module' } },
    { code: '{ await using x = null; }', options: { next: true, sourceType: 'module' } },
    // async function body
    { code: 'async function f() { await using x = null; }', options: { next: true } },
    // for-of and for-await-of heads
    { code: 'for (using x of []) {}', options: { next: true } },
    { code: 'async function f() { for (await using x of []) {} }', options: { next: true } },
    { code: 'for await (using x of []) {}', options: { next: true, sourceType: 'module' } },
    { code: 'for await (await using x of []) {}', options: { next: true, sourceType: 'module' } },
    { code: 'for (using of = null;;) break;', options: { next: true } },
    { code: 'async function f() { for (await using of of []) {} }', options: { next: true } },
    // `using` remains usable as an identifier
    { code: 'using = 1;', options: { next: true, sourceType: 'module' } },
    { code: '{ using[x] = null; }', options: { next: true } },
    { code: 'using\nx = 1;', options: { next: true } },
    // export
    { code: 'export using x = null;', options: { next: true, sourceType: 'module' } },
    { code: 'export await using x = null;', options: { next: true, sourceType: 'module' } },
  ]);

  fail('Using (fail)', [
    // script global scope
    { code: 'using x = null;', options: { next: true } },
    { code: 'using x = null;', options: { next: true, sourceType: 'commonjs' } },
    // await using outside async/module
    { code: 'await using x = null;', options: { next: true } },
    { code: 'function f() { await using x = null; }', options: { next: true, sourceType: 'module' } },
    // missing initializer
    { code: '{ using x; }', options: { next: true } },
    { code: 'async function f() { await using x; }', options: { next: true } },
    // for-in is not allowed
    { code: 'for (using x in y) {}', options: { next: true } },
    { code: 'async function f() { for (await using x in y) {} }', options: { next: true } },
    // destructuring is not allowed
    { code: '{ using [a] = null; }', options: { next: true } },
    { code: '{ using {a} = null; }', options: { next: true } },
    { code: 'async function f() { await using [a] = null; }', options: { next: true } },
    // switch case is not allowed
    { code: 'function f() { switch (x) { case 1: using y = null; } }', options: { next: true } },
  ]);
});

      start: {
        line: start.line,
        column: start.column,
      },
      end: {
        line: parser.tokenLine,
        column: parser.tokenColumn,
      },
    };
    // For Single, start before "//",
    // For HTMLOpen, start before "<!--",
    // For HTMLClose, start before "\n-->"
    parser.options.onComment(
      CommentTypes[type & 0xff],
      source.slice(index, parser.tokenIndex),
      start.index,
      parser.tokenIndex,
      loc,
    );
  }
  return state | LexerState.NewLine;
}

/**
 * Skips multiline comment
 *
 * @param parser Parser object
 * @param state Lexer state
 */
export function skipMultiLineComment(parser: Parser, source: string, state: LexerState): LexerState | void {
  const { index } = parser;
  while (parser.index < parser.end) {
    if (parser.currentChar < 0x2b) {
      let skippedOneAsterisk = false;
      while (parser.currentChar === Chars.Asterisk) {
        if (!skippedOneAsterisk) {
          state &= ~LexerState.LastIsCR;
          skippedOneAsterisk = true;
        }
        if (advanceChar(parser) === Chars.Slash) {
          advanceChar(parser);
          if (parser.options.onComment) {
            const loc = {
              start: {
                line: parser.tokenLine,
                column: parser.tokenColumn,
              },
              end: {
                line: parser.line,
                column: parser.column,
              },
            };
            parser.options.onComment(
              CommentTypes[CommentType.Multi & 0xff],
              source.slice(index, parser.index - 2),
              index - 2, // start before '/*'
              parser.index, // end after '*/'
              loc,
            );
          }
          parser.tokenIndex = parser.index;
          parser.tokenLine = parser.line;
          parser.tokenColumn = parser.column;
          return state;

interface PeekedToken {
  token: Token;
  newLine: boolean;
  hasSpaceBefore: boolean;
}

/**
 * Scans a number of tokens ahead without consuming them.
 */
function peekTokens(parser: Parser, context: Context, count: number): PeekedToken[] {
  const saved = {
    index: parser.index,
    line: parser.line,
    column: parser.column,
    tokenIndex: parser.tokenIndex,
    tokenColumn: parser.tokenColumn,
    tokenLine: parser.tokenLine,
    currentChar: parser.currentChar,
    tokenValue: parser.tokenValue,
    tokenRaw: parser.tokenRaw,
    tokenRegExp: parser.tokenRegExp,
  };

  const result: PeekedToken[] = [];
  const savedOnComment = parser.options.onComment;
  parser.options.onComment = undefined;
  let previousEnd = parser.index;

  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const startLine = parser.line;
    const token = scanSingleToken(parser, context, LexerState.None);
    result.push({


STEP 129 CALL 1 RETURN 0
COMMAND:
cd /app && sed -n '1565,1595p' src/parser.ts
OUTPUT:
    result.push({
      token,
      newLine: parser.line > startLine,
      hasSpaceBefore: parser.tokenIndex > previousEnd,
    });
    previousEnd = parser.index;
  }

  parser.index = saved.index;
  parser.line = saved.line;
  parser.column = saved.column;
  parser.tokenIndex = saved.tokenIndex;
  parser.tokenColumn = saved.tokenColumn;
  parser.tokenLine = saved.tokenLine;
  parser.currentChar = saved.currentChar;
  parser.tokenValue = saved.tokenValue;
  parser.tokenRaw = saved.tokenRaw;
  parser.tokenRegExp = saved.tokenRegExp;
  parser.options.onComment = savedOnComment;

  return result;
}

+/**
+ * Checks whether the token after `using` starts a `using` declaration.
+ */
+function isUsingBindingToken(
+  token: PeekedToken | undefined,
+  next: PeekedToken | undefined,
+  isFor: boolean,
+  isAwait: boolean,
+): boolean {
+  if (!token || token.newLine) return false;
+
+  if ((token.token & Token.IsIdentifier) === Token.IsIdentifier) {
+    // In a for-of/for-await-of head, `for (using of ...)` is interpreted as an
+    // expression headed by the identifier `using` unless the `of` is clearly a
+    // binding identifier (followed by something other than another loop keyword).
+    if (isFor && !isAwait && token.token === Token.OfKeyword) {
+      if (next && (next.token === Token.OfKeyword || next.token === Token.InKeyword)) {
+        return false;
+      }
+    }
+    return true;
+  }
+
+  // `using [x] = null` and `using {x} = null` (with a space) are treated as
+  // declarations, and then rejected because destructuring is not allowed.
+  if ((token.token === Token.LeftBracket || token.token === Token.LeftBrace) && token.hasSpaceBefore) {
+    return true;
+  }
+
+  return false;
+}
+
+/**
+ * Detects `using` / `await using` declarations without consuming tokens.
+ */
+function isUsingDeclarationStart(parser: Parser, context: Context, isAwait: boolean, isFor: boolean): boolean {
+  if (!parser.options.next) return false;
+
+  if (isAwait) {
+    if (parser.getToken() !== Token.AwaitKeyword) return false;
+    const tokens = peekTokens(parser, context, 3);
+    if (tokens.length < 2) return false;
+    if (tokens[0].token !== Token.UsingKeyword || tokens[0].newLine) return false;
+    return isUsingBindingToken(tokens[1], tokens[2], isFor, true);
+  }
+
+  if (parser.getToken() !== Token.UsingKeyword) return false;
+  const tokens = peekTokens(parser, context, 2);
+  return isUsingBindingToken(tokens[0], tokens[1], isFor, false);
+}
+
+/**
+ * Parses a `using` or `await using` declaration.
+ */
+function parseUsingDeclaration(
+  parser: Parser,
+  context: Context,
+  scope: Scope | undefined,
+  privateScope: PrivateScope | undefined,
+  kind: BindingKind,
+  start: Location,
+  isFor: boolean,
+  origin: Origin = isFor ? Origin.ForStatement : Origin.None,
+): ESTree.VariableDeclaration {
+  if (kind & BindingKind.AwaitUsing) {
+    nextToken(parser, context);
+    consume(parser, context, Token.UsingKeyword);
+  } else {
+    nextToken(parser, context);
+  }
+
+  const declarations = parseVariableDeclarationList(
+    parser,
+    isFor ? context | Context.DisallowIn : context,
+    scope,
+    privateScope,
+    kind,
+    origin,
+  );
+
+  if (!isFor) matchOrInsertSemicolon(parser, context | Context.AllowRegExp);
+
+  return parser.finishNode<ESTree.VariableDeclaration>(
+    {
+      type: 'VariableDeclaration',
+      kind: kind & BindingKind.AwaitUsing ? 'await using' : 'using',
+      declarations,
+    },
+    start,
+  );
+}
+
+/**
+ * Parses a `using` / `await using` statement, or falls back to normal
+ * statement parsing when `using`/`await` is used as an identifier.
+ */
+function parseUsingOrAwaitUsingStatement(
+  parser: Parser,
+  context: Context,
+  scope: Scope | undefined,
+  privateScope: PrivateScope | undefined,
+  origin: Origin,
+  labels: ESTree.Labels,
+  isAwait: 0 | 1,
+): ESTree.Statement {
+  if (!isUsingDeclarationStart(parser, context, isAwait === 1, false)) {
+    return parseStatement(parser, context, scope, privateScope, origin, labels, 1);
+  }
+
+  const start = parser.tokenStart;
+  const kind = isAwait ? BindingKind.AwaitUsing : BindingKind.Using;
+
+  if (isAwait && !((context & Context.InAwaitContext) !== 0 || (context & Context.Module && context & Context.InGlobal))) {
+    parser.report(Errors.AwaitUsingOutsideAsync);
+  }
+
+  if ((context & Context.InGlobal) !== 0 && (context & Context.Module) === 0 && (origin & Origin.TopLevel) !== 0) {
+    parser.report(Errors.UsingNotAllowedInGlobal);
+  }
+
+  if (context & Context.InSwitchCase) parser.report(Errors.UsingNotAllowedInSwitch);
+
+  return parseUsingDeclaration(parser, context, scope, privateScope, kind, start, false);
+}

cd /app && cat test/parser/statements/using.ts
OUTPUT:
import { describe } from 'vitest';
import { fail, pass } from '../../test-utils';

describe('Statements - Using', () => {
  pass('Using (pass)', [
    // using declarations in blocks and function bodies
    { code: '{ using x = null; }', options: { next: true } },
    { code: 'function f() { using x = null; }', options: { next: true } },
    { code: '{ using x = null, y = null; }', options: { next: true } },
    // module top-level
    { code: 'using x = null;', options: { next: true, sourceType: 'module' } },
    { code: 'await using x = null;', options: { next: true, sourceType: 'module' } },
    { code: '{ await using x = null; }', options: { next: true, sourceType: 'module' } },
    // async function body
    { code: 'async function f() { await using x = null; }', options: { next: true } },
    // for-of and for-await-of heads
    { code: 'for (using x of []) {}', options: { next: true } },
    { code: 'for (using x of []) {}', options: { next: true, sourceType: 'module' } },
    { code: 'async function f() { for (await using x of []) {} }', options: { next: true } },
    { code: 'for (await using x of []) {}', options: { next: true, sourceType: 'module' } },
    { code: 'for await (using x of []) {}', options: { next: true, sourceType: 'module' } },
    { code: 'for await (await using x of []) {}', options: { next: true, sourceType: 'module' } },
    { code: 'for (using x = null;;) break;', options: { next: true } },
    { code: 'for (using of = null;;) break;', options: { next: true } },
    { code: 'async function f() { for (await using of of []) {} }', options: { next: true } },
    // a block inside a switch case is a valid container
    { code: 'function f() { switch (x) { case 1: { using y = null; } } }', options: { next: true } },
    // `using` remains usable as an identifier
    { code: 'using = 1;', options: { next: true, sourceType: 'module' } },
    { code: 'using = 1;', options: { sourceType: 'module' } },
    { code: '{ using[x] = null; }', options: { next: true } },
    { code: 'using\nx = 1;', options: { next: true } },
    // export
    { code: 'export using x = null;', options: { next: true, sourceType: 'module' } },
    { code: 'export await using x = null;', options: { next: true, sourceType: 'module' } },
  ]);

  fail('Using (fail)', [
    // script global scope
    { code: 'using x = null;', options: { next: true } },
    { code: 'using x = null;', options: { next: true, sourceType: 'commonjs' } },
    // await using outside async/module
    { code: 'await using x = null;', options: { next: true } },
    { code: 'await using x = null;', options: { next: true, sourceType: 'commonjs' } },
    { code: 'function f() { await using x = null; }', options: { next: true, sourceType: 'module' } },
    // missing initializer
    { code: '{ using x; }', options: { next: true } },
    { code: 'async function f() { await using x; }', options: { next: true } },
    // for-in is not allowed
    { code: 'for (using x in y) {}', options: { next: true } },
    { code: 'async function f() { for (await using x in y) {} }', options: { next: true } },
    // destructuring is not allowed
    { code: '{ using [a] = null; }', options: { next: true } },
    { code: '{ using [] = null; }', options: { next: true } },
    { code: '{ using {a} = null; }', options: { next: true } },
    { code: 'async function f() { await using [a] = null; }', options: { next: true } },
    { code: 'async function f() { await using [] = null; }', options: { next: true } },
    // switch case is not allowed
    { code: 'function f() { switch (x) { case 1: using y = null; } }', options: { next: true } },
    // `let` cannot be a using binding
    { code: '{ using let = null; }', options: { next: true } },
    { code: 'async function f() { await using let = null; }', options: { next: true } },
  ]);
});
